	protected void DrawWoF()
	{
		if (wofNPCIndex < 0 || !npc[wofNPCIndex].active || npc[wofNPCIndex].life <= 0)
		{
			return;
		}
		for (int i = 0; i < 255; i++)

		DrawWOFBody();
	}

	private static void DrawWOFBody()
	{
		int num = TextureAssets.Wof.Height() / 3;
		float num2 = wofDrawAreaTop;
		float num3 = wofDrawAreaBottom;
		num3 = screenPosition.Y + (float)screenHeight;
		float num4 = (int)((num2 - screenPosition.Y) / (float)num) + 1;

		{

		}
		float num5 = num4 * (float)num;
		if (num5 > 0f)
		{
			num2 -= num5;
		}
		float num6 = npc[wofNPCIndex].position.X;
		if (npc[wofNPCIndex].direction > 0)
		{
			num6 -= 80f;
		}
		SpriteEffects effects = SpriteEffects.None;
		if (npc[wofNPCIndex].spriteDirection == 1)
		{
			effects = SpriteEffects.FlipHorizontally;
		}
		int num7 = wofDrawFrameIndex / 6 * num;
		if (!gamePaused && ++wofDrawFrameIndex >= 18)
		{
			wofDrawFrameIndex = 0;
		}
		float num8 = num3 - num2;
		for (int i = (int)num2; (float)i < num3; i += num)
		{
			num8 = num3 - (float)i;
			if (num8 > (float)num)
			{
				num8 = num;
			}
			for (int j = 0; (float)j < num8; j += 16)
			{
				int x = (int)(num6 + (float)(TextureAssets.Wof.Width() / 2)) / 16;
				int y = (i + j) / 16;
				spriteBatch.Draw(TextureAssets.Wof.Value, new Vector2(num6 - screenPosition.X, (float)(i + j) - screenPosition.Y), new Microsoft.Xna.Framework.Rectangle(0, num7 + j, TextureAssets.Wof.Width(), 16), Lighting.GetColor(x, y), 0f, default(Vector2), 1f, effects, 0f);
			}
		}
	}

	private static void DrawWOFRopeToTheHungry(int i)
	{
		float num = npc[wofNPCIndex].position.X + (float)(npc[wofNPCIndex].width / 2);
		float y = npc[wofNPCIndex].position.Y;
		float num2 = wofDrawAreaBottom - wofDrawAreaTop;
		bool flag = false;
		if (npc[i].frameCounter > 7.0)
		{
			flag = true;
		}
		y = (float)wofDrawAreaTop + num2 * npc[i].ai[0];
		float scale = npc[i].scale;
		float x = npc[i].Center.X;
		float y2 = npc[i].Bottom.Y - (float)npc[i].height * 0.5f * scale;
		Vector2 vector = new Vector2(x, y2);
		vector += npc[i].netOffset;
		float num3 = num - vector.X;
		float num4 = y - vector.Y;
		float rotation = (float)Math.Atan2(num4, num3) - 1.57f;
		bool flag2 = true;
		while (flag2)
		{
			SpriteEffects effects = SpriteEffects.None;
			if (flag)
			{
				effects = SpriteEffects.FlipHorizontally;
				flag = false;
			}
			else
			{
				flag = true;
			}
			int height = 28;
			int num5 = (int)(40f * scale);
			float num6 = (float)Math.Sqrt(num3 * num3 + num4 * num4);
			if (num6 < (float)num5)
			{
				height = (int)num6 - num5 + 28;
				flag2 = false;
			}
			num6 = 28f * scale / num6;
			num3 *= num6;
			num4 *= num6;
			vector.X += num3;
			vector.Y += num4;
			num3 = num - vector.X;
			num4 = y - vector.Y;
			Microsoft.Xna.Framework.Color color = Lighting.GetColor((int)vector.X / 16, (int)(vector.Y / 16f));
			spriteBatch.Draw(TextureAssets.Chain12.Value, new Vector2(vector.X - screenPosition.X, vector.Y - screenPosition.Y), new Microsoft.Xna.Framework.Rectangle(0, 0, TextureAssets.Chain4.Width(), height), color, rotation, new Vector2((float)TextureAssets.Chain4.Width() * 0.5f, (float)TextureAssets.Chain4.Height() * 0.5f), scale, effects, 0f);
		}
	}

	private static void DrawWOFTongueToPlayer(int i)
	{
		float num = npc[wofNPCIndex].position.X + (float)(npc[wofNPCIndex].width / 2);
		float num2 = npc[wofNPCIndex].position.Y + (float)(npc[wofNPCIndex].height / 2);
		Vector2 vector = new Vector2(player[i].position.X + (float)player[i].width * 0.5f, player[i].position.Y + (float)player[i].height * 0.5f);
		float num3 = num - vector.X;
		float num4 = num2 - vector.Y;
		float rotation = (float)Math.Atan2(num4, num3) - 1.57f;
		bool flag = true;
		while (flag)
		{
			float num5 = (float)Math.Sqrt(num3 * num3 + num4 * num4);
			if (num5 < 40f)
			{
				flag = false;
				continue;
			}
			num5 = (float)TextureAssets.Chain12.Height() / num5;
			num3 *= num5;
			num4 *= num5;
			vector.X += num3;
			vector.Y += num4;
			num3 = num - vector.X;
			num4 = num2 - vector.Y;
			Microsoft.Xna.Framework.Color color = Lighting.GetColor((int)vector.X / 16, (int)(vector.Y / 16f));
			spriteBatch.Draw(TextureAssets.Chain12.Value, new Vector2(vector.X - screenPosition.X, vector.Y - screenPosition.Y), new Microsoft.Xna.Framework.Rectangle(0, 0, TextureAssets.Chain12.Width(), TextureAssets.Chain12.Height()), color, rotation, new Vector2((float)TextureAssets.Chain12.Width() * 0.5f, (float)TextureAssets.Chain12.Height() * 0.5f), 1f, SpriteEffects.None, 0f);
		}

28	interface EyeLocals { charge: number; burst: number; lastHeadHp: number } // localAI[1]/[2] + 血量同步基线

80	export function spawnWOF(game: GameHooks, px: number, py: number): Enemy | null {
81	  const st = game.world.store;
82	  // :81114 高度门 + 已在场门（Main.wofNPCIndex>=0 ≙ AnyNPCs(113)）
83	  if (py / TILE < st.h - 205) return null;

86	  const num = px / TILE > st.w / 2 ? -1 : 1;
87	  // :81124-81141 落位扫描

91	    let flag = true;
92	    if (player && !player.dead && player.x > num2 - 1200 && player.x < num2 + 1200) {
93	      num2 -= num * TILE;
94	      flag = false;
95	    }
96	    if (num2 / TILE < 20 || num2 / TILE > st.w - 20) flag = true;
97	    if (flag) break;
98	  }

101	  let num5 = Math.floor(py / TILE);
102	  const tileAir = (x: number, y: number): boolean =>

104	  if (!st.inBounds(num4, num5) || st.isSolid(num4, num5)
105	    || (st.inBounds(num4, num5 - 0) && st.liquid[st.idx(num4, num5 - 0)] >= 100)) {

127	      .announce?.('Announcement.HasAwoken', name, 175, 75, 255);

132	/** 原版 UnderworldLayer = maxTilesY-200(World.lavaLine,TerrainPass 设定;0=未知回退) */
133	const underworldLayer = (game: GameHooks): number => {

138	/** 朝 (tx,ty) 的喷口旋转角(墙/之眼共用,:26050-26079 / :26183-26206):
139	 *  dx/dy 先乘回距离(原版 num387*=num389,喂 atan2 不归一),玩家在推进方向前侧才瞄准,
140	 *  否则贴回 0(嘴/眼朝前)。返回 [rotation, flag](flag=是否在前侧) */
141	function mouthRotation(e: Enemy, player: { cx: number; cy: number }): [number, boolean] {
142	  let dx = player.cx - e.cx, dy = player.cy - e.cy;
143	  const dist = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
144	  dx *= dist; dy *= dist;
145	  if (e.facing > 0) {
146	    if (player.cx > e.cx) return [Math.atan2(-dy, -dx) + Math.PI, true];
147	    return [0, false];
148	  }
149	  if (player.cx < e.cx) return [Math.atan2(dy, dx) + Math.PI, true];
150	  return [0, false];
151	}

158	 *  移动自行积分:Y 直接吸附(velocity.Y 分支被 :25977 覆盖),X 沿 vx 推进。 */
159	export function wallOfFleshAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {

162	  // 转化——bossFled 防 Game 击杀流误记,2026-08-13）
163	  if (e.x < 160 || e.x > (st.w - 10) * TILE) { e.bossFled = true; e.dead = true; return; }

166	  if (e.ai3 === 0) { e.ai3 = 1; wofDrawAreaBottom = -1; wofDrawAreaTop = -1; }

171	  e.ai1 += 1;
172	  if (e.ai2 === 0) {

174	    if (e.hp < e.maxHp * 0.2) e.ai1 += 1;

186	    if (countType(game, 117) < 10) {
187	      const leech = spawnPart(game, e, 117); // 117 aiStyle 6 蠕虫,已有实现
188	      if (leech) {
189	        // :25818 坐标是底部中心(NewNPC Bottom 锚点,NPC.cs:81547):中心 X、墙中心下 20
190	        leech.x = e.cx - leech.w / 2;
191	        leech.y = e.y + e.h / 2 + 20 - leech.h;
192	        leech.vx = e.facing * 8; // :25820 沿推进方向甩出
193	        // 体节链（AI_006 :51706-51749）：头生成时 ai[2]=Next(3,6) → 3-5 节身 118
194	        // + 尾节 119（身节 ai[2]>0 逐节克隆、归零换尾）——spawnWormChainFor 即
195	        // "body×n + 尾"结构，n 取同一掷骰区间（Enemy 为 type-only 导入，经代理出口）
196	        spawnWormChainFor(game, leech, 3 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 3));
197	      }
198	    }
199	  }
200	  // ---- :25800-25804 localAI[3] 尖啸计时(600-1600t 随机,负值冷却) ----
201	  L.roarT += 1;
202	  if (L.roarT >= 600 + Math.random() * 1000) {
203	    L.roarT = -Math.random() * 200;
204	    // :25803 PlaySound(4, x, y, 10) = Sounds/Zombie_10（LegacySoundPlayer.cs:221
205	    // "Zombie_"+k）——WoF 专属尖啸，非 Boss Roar
206	    game.playSfxFiles(['Zombie_10'], 0.8, e.cx, e.cy);
207	  }
208	
209	  // ---- 地狱空腔上下沿扫描(:25837-25965) ----
210	  const underworld = underworldLayer(game);       // Main.UnderworldLayer
211	  const num372 = underworld + 10;                 // 允许带顶部
212	  const num373 = num372 + 70;                     // 允许带底部(共 70 格)
213	  const num374 = Math.floor(e.x / TILE);          // 墙左沿 tile
214	  const num375 = Math.floor((e.x + e.w) / TILE);  // 墙右沿 tile
215	  const num376 = Math.floor((e.y + e.h / 2) / TILE); // 墙中心行
216	  // 单行内实心/液体 tile 计数(:25866-25877 / :25902-25913;原版 try/catch 出界 +15 不移植)
217	  const rowHits = (y: number): number => {
218	    let c = 0;
219	    for (let x = num374; x <= num375; x++) {
220	      if (st.inBounds(x, y) && (st.isSolid(x, y) || st.liquid[st.idx(x, y)] > 0)) c++;
221	    }
222	    return c;
223	  };
224	  // 底沿:从中心行 +7 向下累计 15 个实心/液体(:25851-25882)
225	  let hits = 0;
226	  let yScan = num376 + 7;
227	  while (hits < 15 && yScan > underworld) {
228	    yScan++;
229	    if (yScan > st.h - 10) { yScan = st.h - 10; break; }
230	    if (yScan < num372) continue;
231	    hits += rowHits(yScan);
232	  }
233	  yScan += 4; // 再让出 4 格
234	  wofDrawAreaBottom = approachPx(wofDrawAreaBottom, yScan * TILE); // :25884-25895 每 tick ±1 平滑
235	  // 顶沿:从中心行 -7 向上累计 15 个(:25897-25935)
236	  hits = 0;
237	  yScan = num376 - 7;
238	  while (hits < 15 && yScan < st.h - 10) {
239	    yScan--;
240	    if (yScan <= 10) { yScan = 10; break; }
241	    if (yScan > num373) continue;
242	    if (yScan < num372) { yScan = num372; break; }
243	    hits += rowHits(yScan);
244	  }
245	  yScan -= 4;
246	  wofDrawAreaTop = approachPx(wofDrawAreaTop, yScan * TILE); // :25918-25929
247	  // :25930-25939 钳到允许带 [num372, num373]
248	  wofDrawAreaTop = Math.round(clamp(wofDrawAreaTop, num372 * TILE, num373 * TILE));
249	  wofDrawAreaBottom = Math.round(clamp(wofDrawAreaBottom, num372 * TILE, num373 * TILE));
250	  // :25940-25946 上下沿间距保底 160px
251	  if (wofDrawAreaTop > wofDrawAreaBottom - 160) wofDrawAreaTop = wofDrawAreaBottom - 160;
252	  else if (wofDrawAreaBottom < wofDrawAreaTop + 160) wofDrawAreaBottom = wofDrawAreaTop + 160;
253	
254	  // ---- Y 吸附空腔竖直中点(:25948-25977;velocity.Y 的 ±1 分支随即被覆盖,语义即吸附) ----
255	  const num381 = (wofDrawAreaBottom + wofDrawAreaTop) / 2 - e.h / 2;
256	  e.y = num381;
257	
258	  // ---- 推进速度(:25979-25994 经典档):1.5 起,血量阈值逐档加成 ----
259	  let num382 = 1.5;
260	  if (e.hp < e.maxHp * 0.75) num382 += 0.25;
261	  if (e.hp < e.maxHp * 0.5) num382 += 0.4;
262	  if (e.hp < e.maxHp * 0.25) num382 += 0.5;
263	  if (e.hp < e.maxHp * 0.1) num382 += 0.6;
264	  // :25995-26011 expert(<66%+0.3/<33%+0.3/<5%+0.6/<3.5%+0.6,整体 ×1.35+0.35)与
265	  //        getGoodWorld(×1.1+0.2)分支:不实现
266	
267	  // ---- 推进方向(:26013-26029):vx==0(首帧)按玩家侧定向;此后恒沿 direction 定速 ----
268	  if (e.vx === 0) {
269	    // TargetClosest(:26014;玩家死亡时改选存活玩家侧,:26016-26027 多人分支不适用)
270	    e.facing = player && player.cx >= e.cx ? 1 : -1;
271	    e.vx = e.facing;
272	  }
273	  if (e.vx < 0) { e.vx = -num382; e.facing = -1; }
274	  else { e.vx = num382; e.facing = 1; }
275	
276	  // ---- 玩家死亡 → 3 秒自毁离场(:26031-26048 localAI[1] 1/180 爬满即死) ----
277	  if (!player || player.dead) {
278	    L.despawnT += 1 / 180;
279	    if (L.despawnT >= 1) {
280	      // :26027 PlaySound(4, x, y, 10) = Zombie_10 尖啸（同周期吼）
281	      game.playSfxFiles(['Zombie_10'], 0.8, e.cx, e.cy);
282	      e.hp = 0;
283	      e.bossFled = true;   // 玩家死 3s 自毁（:26040-26048 life=0 静默,非击杀）
284	      e.dead = true;
285	      return;
286	    }
287	  } else {
288	    L.despawnT = clamp(L.despawnT - 1 / 30, 0, 1);
289	  }
290	
291	  // ---- 喷口朝向(:26050-26079) ----
292	  if (player) e.spin = mouthRotation(e, player)[0];
293	
294	  // ---- :26081-26125 expert 模式 Hungry 重生：每 tick 按墙血量掷骰，重生一只 115
295	  //      到未占用的 ai0 槽位（0.05..0.95 十槽）。血越低 num391 越小 → 概率越高 ----
296	  if (mainExpertMode(game.world)) {
297	    // :26083-26093 num391 = (int)(1 + life/lifeMax*10)²，逐级下限托底后 ×0.7
298	    let num391 = Math.trunc(1 + (e.hp / e.maxHp) * 10);
299	    num391 *= num391;
300	    if (num391 < 400) num391 = Math.trunc((num391 * 19 + 400) / 20);
301	    if (num391 < 60) num391 = Math.trunc((num391 * 3 + 60) / 4);
302	    if (num391 < 20) num391 = Math.trunc((num391 + 20) / 2);
303	    num391 = Math.trunc(num391 * 0.7);
304	    // :26095 Main.rand.Next(num391)==0 → 进入槽位挑选
305	    if (Math.floor(Math.random() * num391) === 0) {
306	      // :26097-26106 收集在场 115 的 ai0（≤10 只）
307	      const used: number[] = [];
308	      for (const n of game.enemies() as Enemy[]) {
309	        if (used.length < 10 && !n.dead && n.vanillaId === 115) used.push(n.ai0);
310	      }
311	      // :26107-26109 槽越多越难再添（1+2n 分母，≤1 才放行）
312	      if (used.length < 10 && Math.floor(Math.random() * (1 + used.length * 2)) <= 1) {
313	        // :26110-26120 挑未占用的槽号（1000 次兜底）
314	        let num394 = -1;
315	        for (let num395 = 0; num395 < 1000; num395++) {
316	          const num396 = Math.floor(Math.random() * 10);
317	          if (!used.includes(num396 * 0.1 - 0.05)) { num394 = num396; break; }
318	        }
319	        if (num394 >= 0) {
320	          // :26122 NewNPC(position.X, num381, 115, ai0=num394*0.1-0.05)——Bottom 锚点
321	          const h = spawnPart(game, e, 115);
322	          if (h) {
323	            h.x = e.x;
324	            h.y = num381 - h.h;
325	            h.ai0 = num394 * 0.1 - 0.05;
326	            h.ai3 = e.id;      // 头锚定槽（文件头结构性差异 1）
327	            h.bInit = true;
328	          }
329	        }
330	      }
331	    }
332	  }
333	
334	  // ---- 首帧部件生成(:26127-26140 localAI[0]==1 时执行一次):
335	  //      双眼 114(上眼 ai0=1 贴顶沿半程 / 下眼 ai0=-1 贴底沿半程)+ 11 只 Hungry 115
336	  //      (ai0 = k*0.1-0.05 沿空腔竖向均布)。眼 ai1 / Hungry ai3 = 头实体 id 锚定
337	  //      (见文件头说明与 hungryAI 注释) ----
338	  if (e.ai3 === 1) {
339	    e.ai3 = 2;
340	    // 生成坐标均为底部中心(NewNPC Bottom 锚点,NPC.cs:81547):X = 墙左沿,Y = 给定值
341	    const eyeTop = spawnPart(game, e, 114);
342	    if (eyeTop) {
343	      eyeTop.x = e.x; eyeTop.y = (e.cy + wofDrawAreaTop) / 2 - eyeTop.h;
344	      eyeTop.ai0 = 1; eyeTop.ai1 = e.id; eyeTop.bInit = true;
345	    }
346	    const eyeBottom = spawnPart(game, e, 114);
347	    if (eyeBottom) {
348	      eyeBottom.x = e.x; eyeBottom.y = (e.cy + wofDrawAreaBottom) / 2 - eyeBottom.h;
349	      eyeBottom.ai0 = -1; eyeBottom.ai1 = e.id; eyeBottom.bInit = true;
350	    }
351	    const hungryY = (e.cy + wofDrawAreaBottom) / 2;
352	    for (let k = 0; k < 11; k++) {
353	      const h = spawnPart(game, e, 115);
354	      if (h) { h.x = e.x; h.y = hungryY - h.h; h.ai0 = k * 0.1 - 0.05; h.ai3 = e.id; h.bInit = true; }
355	    }
356	  }
357	
358	  // 水平推进(AI 自行积分;Y 已吸附,不积分)
359	  e.x += e.vx;
360	}
361	
362	/** draw 区域每 tick ±1 平滑逼近(:25884-25895 / :25918-25929;首次直接取值) */
363	function approachPx(cur: number, target: number): number {
364	  if (cur === -1) return target;
365	  if (cur > target) { cur--; if (cur < target) cur = target; }
366	  else if (cur < target) { cur++; if (cur > target) cur = target; }
367	  return cur;
368	}
369	
370	// ================= aiStyle 28 血肉墙之眼 114(NPC.cs:26141-26298) =================
371	
372	/** ai0 = ±1(1=上眼 -1=下眼),ai1 = 头实体 id。
373	 *  X 钉在墙上、Y 在空腔上半/下半程的中点吸附(±1 步进,±5 钳);
374	 *  蓄能 600t 后进入激光连射(经典 4 连,低血加档;45t 间隔 + 视线检查),
375	 *  眼激光 Projectile 83(速 9 起/伤 11 起,低血加档)。血量与墙共享(realLife)。 */
376	export function wofEyeAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {
377	  const head = headOf(e, game);
378	  // :26142-26146 Main.wofNPCIndex<0(墙已不在)→ 自毁
379	  if (!head) { e.dead = true; return; }
380	  const L = eyeLocalOf(e);
381	  // :26147-26152 realLife 血量共享:眼受到的伤害回流墙(原版 StrikeNPC 按 realLife 转移),
382	  // 再镜像墙血量。回流判定:仅当墙自身血量本 tick 未变(head.hp == 上次同步值)且眼血
383	  // 低于同步值时才算"眼的独立伤害"(墙血只单调降,被回补/改档时不得误判)
384	  if (head.hp === L.lastHeadHp && e.hp < head.hp) head.hp = e.hp;
385	  L.lastHeadHp = head.hp;
386	  if (head.hp > 0) { e.hp = head.hp; e.maxHp = head.maxHp; }
387	  // :26149 realLife = Main.wofNPCIndex 的连带结算语义：打眼 = StrikeNPC 转发到墙
388	  //（NPC.StrikeNPC :82224+ realLife 转移走**墙的防御**结算）→ 眼的受击防御取墙
389	  // def12（数据 114 自身 defense=0，不镜像会让打眼比打墙每击多算 6 点）
390	  e.def.defense = head.def.defense ?? 0;
391	
392	  // :26157-26159 X 钉在墙左沿,朝向随墙
393	  e.x = head.x;
394	  e.facing = head.facing;
395	  // :26161-26167 Y 目标:空腔中点向上/下半程取中(上眼贴顶 / 下眼贴底)
396	  const mid = (wofDrawAreaBottom + wofDrawAreaTop) / 2;
397	  const ty = (e.ai0 > 0 ? (mid + wofDrawAreaTop) / 2 : (mid + wofDrawAreaBottom) / 2) - e.h / 2;
398	  if (e.y > ty + 1) e.vy = -1;
399	  else if (e.y < ty - 1) e.vy = 1;
400	  else { e.vy = 0; e.y = ty; }
401	  e.vy = clamp(e.vy, -5, 5); // :26177-26182
402	
403	  // :26183-26206 喷口朝向(仅玩家在前侧时瞄准)
404	  let front = false;
405	  if (player) [e.spin, front] = mouthRotation(e, player);
406	
407	  // ---- 激光(:26211-26296) ----
408	  // :26213-26235 连射发数与蓄能速率随墙血量加档(经典)
409	  let num409 = 4;   // 一轮发数
410	  L.charge += 1;
411	  if (head.hp < head.maxHp * 0.75) { L.charge += 1; num409++; }
412	  if (head.hp < head.maxHp * 0.5) { L.charge += 1; num409++; }
413	  if (head.hp < head.maxHp * 0.25) { L.charge += 1; num409 += 2; }
414	  if (head.hp < head.maxHp * 0.1) { L.charge += 2; num409 += 3; }
415	  // :26236-26242 expert(整体 +1 档,<10% 再 +3):不实现
416	  if (L.burst === 0) {
417	    // 蓄能段(:26245-26249):满 600t → 进连射
418	    if (L.charge > 600) { L.burst = 1; L.charge = 0; }
419	  } else if (player && L.charge > 45 && losClear(game, e.x, e.y, player.x, player.y)) {
420	    // 连射段(:26252-26262):45t 一发,需视线(Collision.CanHit);发满一轮回蓄能
421	    L.charge = 0;
422	    L.burst += 1;
423	    if (L.burst >= num409) L.burst = 0;
424	    if (front) {
425	      // :26264-26296 眼激光 83:速 9 / 伤 11 起,低血加档(:26268-26276)
426	      let num410 = 9;
427	      let num411 = 11;
428	      if (head.hp < head.maxHp * 0.5) { num411++; num410 += 1; }
429	      if (head.hp < head.maxHp * 0.25) { num411++; num410 += 1; }
430	      if (head.hp < head.maxHp * 0.1) { num411 += 2; num410 += 2; }
431	      const dx = player.cx - e.cx, dy = player.cy - e.cy;
432	      const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
433	      const vx = (dx / d) * num410, vy = (dy / d) * num410;
434	      // 出膛点 = 眼中心 + 单位速度(:26278-26286);83 SetDefaults：timeLeft 600 /
435	      // extraUpdates 2（Projectile.cs:1360-1370）——extraUpdates 由 Arrow 按
436	      // projId 缺省注入（PROJ_EXTRA_UPDATES），此处不传
437	      shoot(game, e.cx + vx, e.cy + vy, vx, vy, num411, 83, 600);
438	    }
439	  }
440	
441	  // 移动自行积分:X 已钉墙,仅 Y 步进
442	  e.y += e.vy;
443	}
444	
445	// ================= aiStyle 29 饥饿者 115(NPC.cs:26299-26496) =================
446	
447	/** ai0 = 沿空腔竖向的挂点比例(墙生成时 -0.05..0.95 均布),ai1 = 脱落倒计时(0=挂墙),
448	 *  ai2 = 环绕半径爆发计时(100-200 内 ×1.3,200 复位)。ai3 = 头实体 id 锚定
449	 *  (原版无此槽语义,靠 Main.wofNPCIndex;见文件头说明)。
450	 *  挂墙(ai1==0):绕锚点(墙中心 X, 空腔顶 + 跨度×ai0)以 ±0.1 步进追"锚点 + 朝玩家
451	 *  偏移(钳到半径 num415)",速度钳 ±4(经典);受击 → ai1=10 脱落数 tick 自由飞行;
452	 *  墙血 <75%/<50% 时半径 300→500→700、伤害 45/60、防御 20/30。 */
453	export function hungryAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {
454	  // :26299-26301 justHit（受击单帧旗标，hurt() 置位）→ ai[1]=10（脱落 10t 后回挂墙态）
455	  if (e.justHit) e.ai1 = 10;
456	  const head = headOf(e, game);
457	  // :26303-26306 Main.wofNPCIndex<0(墙已不在)→ 自毁
458	  if (!head) { e.dead = true; return; }
459	  // ai0 初值 -1120(史莱姆语义)复位:非墙生成的 Hungry 挂空腔中点
460	  if (!e.bInit) { e.bInit = true; if (e.ai0 < -1000) e.ai0 = 0.5; }
461	
462	  // :26308-26335 挂墙半径/伤害/防御随墙血量变化；<50%：伤害 60 防 30（经典半径 700，
463	  //   专家半径不变但步进 num414+0.066）；<75%：伤害 45 防 20（经典半径 500 / 专家 +0.033）
464	  //   ——注意半径改值被【经典档门】包住，专家档走步进加成
465	  const expert = mainExpertMode(game.world);   // Main.expertMode（Main.cs:2762）
466	  let num414 = 0.1;   // 步进
467	  let num415 = 300;   // 挂墙半径
468	  const b = baseOf(e);
469	  e.def.damage = b.dmg; // :26311 damage = defDamage(每 tick 复位)
470	  let num416 = 0;
471	  if (head.hp < head.maxHp * 0.5) {
472	    num416 = 60;
473	    e.def.defense = 30;
474	    if (!expert) num415 = 700;
475	    else num414 += 0.066;
476	  } else if (head.hp < head.maxHp * 0.75) {
477	    num416 = 45;
478	    e.def.defense = 20;
479	    if (!expert) num415 = 500;
480	    else num414 += 0.033;
481	  } else {
482	    e.def.defense = b.def;
483	  }
484	  // :26333-26335 damage = GetAttackDamage_ScaledByDifficulty(num416)（EnemyDamageMultiplier）
485	  if (num416 > 0) e.def.damage = getAttackDamageScaledByDifficulty(num416, e.difficulty);
486	  // :26337-26358 专家档尾段：①defense = defDefense（撤销上面的 30/20 血量防修正）；
487	  //   ②挂墙半径按 whoAmI 槽位乘： %4==0 ×1.75、%4==1 ×1.5、%4==2 ×1.25（%4==3 不乘），
488	  //   再 %3==0 ×1.5、%3==1 ×1.25，最后整体 ×0.75。本仓无 NPC 槽位 → 用实体 id 取模
489	  //   （Entity.id 递增发放，槽位分布近似，结构性差异已在文件头登记）
490	  if (expert) {
491	    e.def.defense = b.def;
492	    if (e.id % 4 === 0) num415 *= 1.75;
493	    if (e.id % 4 === 1) num415 *= 1.5;
494	    if (e.id % 4 === 2) num415 *= 1.25;
495	    if (e.id % 3 === 0) num415 *= 1.5;
496	    if (e.id % 3 === 1) num415 *= 1.25;
497	    num415 *= 0.75;
498	  }
499	
500	  // ---- 锚点(:26360-26362):墙中心 X,空腔顶 + 跨度×ai0 ----
501	  const num417 = head.cx;
502	  const y2 = wofDrawAreaTop + (wofDrawAreaBottom - wofDrawAreaTop) * e.ai0;
503	  // :26364-26370 爆发计时:101-200 内半径 ×1.3(取整),200 复位
504	  e.ai2 += 1;
505	  if (e.ai2 > 100) {
506	    num415 = Math.floor(num415 * 1.3);
507	    if (e.ai2 > 200) e.ai2 = 0;
508	  }
509	  // ---- 朝玩家偏移(:26372-26377;无玩家时偏移 0 = 回挂点) ----
510	  let num419 = (player ? player.cx : e.cx) - e.w / 2 - num417;
511	  let num420 = (player ? player.cy : e.cy) - e.h / 2 - y2;
512	  const num421 = Math.sqrt(num419 * num419 + num420 * num420);
513	  if (e.ai1 === 0) {

			if (frame.Y >= num * Main.npcFrameCount[type])
			{
				frame.Y = 0;
			}
			break;

152	  }

1424	  /** 受伤实体血条（DrawInterface_14_EntityHealthBars Main.cs:45203 + DrawHealthBar :21748）：
1425	   *  life != lifeMax 即显示（无时间衰减——原版打到没满血就一直显示）；画在脚底
1426	   *  +10+NPCAddHeight；填充 36px×比例；绿→黄→红渐变（:21766-21778）；光照亮度调
1427	   *  alpha（:45417 Brightness）。蠕虫段不画（头部血条代表全链）；毁灭者原版为
1428	   *  全链共享一条（destroyerHB 平滑），此处近似画在头下。专家档克脑 266 不画
1429	   *  （Main.cs:45321 `(!expertMode || type != 266)` 门——有四镜像在,血条会穿帮）。 */
1430	  private drawHealthBar(e: { cx: number; cy: number; y: number; h: number; hp: number; maxHp: number; vanillaId: number | null; wormFollow?: Enemy['wormFollow'] }, expert = false) {
1431	    const hp = e.hp, maxHp = e.maxHp;
1432	    if (hp <= 0 || hp >= maxHp) return;
1433	    if (e.wormFollow) return;
1434	    if (expert && e.vanillaId === 266) return;   // Main.cs:45321
1435	    if (!this.atlas) return;
1436	    const hb1 = this.atlas.ensureVImage('vanilla/HealthBar1.png');
1437	    const hb2 = this.atlas.ensureVImage('vanilla/HealthBar2.png');
1438	    if (!hb1 || !hb2) return;
1439	    const vid = e.vanillaId ?? 0;
1440	    const scale = Renderer.HB_BIG_SCALE.has(vid) ? 1.5 : 1;
1441	    const x = e.cx - 18 * scale;
1442	    const y = e.y + e.h + 10 + (Renderer.HB_ADD_H[vid] ?? 0);
1443	    const fill = Math.max(0, Math.min(36, Math.floor((36 * hp) / maxHp)));  // num2
1444	    // 光照 alpha（Brightness = 中心格最大通道/255；8 档量化控 tint 缓存规模）
1445	    let aQ = 1;
1446	    if (this.lighting) {
1447	      const [lr, lg, lb] = this.lighting.lightAt(Math.floor(e.cx / TILE), Math.floor(e.cy / TILE));
1448	      aQ = Math.round((Math.max(lr, lg, lb) / 255) * 7) / 7;
1449	    }
1450	    if (aQ <= 0) aQ = 0.15; // 全黑也留一点可见度（光照区外 lightAt=0 的兜底）
1451	    // 颜色（:21766-21778）：n=hp%-0.1；>0.5 绿满红减，否则红满绿增
1452	    let n = hp / maxHp - 0.1;
1453	    n = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, n));
1454	    const r = Math.round(n > 0.5 ? 255 * (1 - n) * 2 : 255);
1455	    const g = Math.round(n > 0.5 ? 255 : 255 * n * 2);
1456	    const tint = (img: CanvasImageSource, tex: string, sx: number, sw: number) =>
1457	      this.tintedSprite(`hb${tex}|f${fill}|a${aQ}`, { img, sx, sy: 0, sw, sh: 12 }, [r, g, 0]);
1458	    const ctx = this.ctx;
1459	    ctx.globalAlpha = 0.95 * aQ;
1460	    if (fill < 34) {
1461	      // :21814-21828：背景两段（fill 处 2px 帽 + 余量）+ 填充两段（主体 + 末端 2px）
1462	      ctx.drawImage(tint(hb2, '2', 2, 2), x + fill * scale, y, 2 * scale, 12 * scale);
1463	      ctx.drawImage(tint(hb2, '2', fill + 2, 36 - fill - 2), x + (fill + 2) * scale, y, (36 - fill - 2) * scale, 12 * scale);
1464	      if (fill > 2) ctx.drawImage(tint(hb1, '1', 0, fill - 2), x, y, (fill - 2) * scale, 12 * scale);
1465	      ctx.drawImage(tint(hb1, '1', 32, 2), x + (fill - 2) * scale, y, 2 * scale, 12 * scale);
1466	    } else {
1467	      // :21830-21836：fill≥34 全长填充 + 尾部背景
1468	      if (fill < 36) ctx.drawImage(tint(hb2, '2', fill, 36 - fill), x + fill * scale, y, (36 - fill) * scale, 12 * scale);
1469	      ctx.drawImage(tint(hb1, '1', 0, fill), x, y, fill * scale, 12 * scale);
1470	    }
1471	    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
1472	  }

1474	  private drawCritter(c: Critter) {
1475	    if (!this.atlas) return;
1476	    const meta = this.atlas.vnpcMeta(c.def.npc);
1477	    if (!meta) return;
1478	    // 帧选择优先级：分段动画（鸭子） > 鸟类栖息末帧 > 蝴蝶物种窗口 > 全表循环
1479	    let frame: number;
1480	    const anim = c.def.anim;
1481	    if (anim) {
1482	      // 状态选段（游水只认滞回锁：flying 后 inWater 冻结的历史 bug 不会再影响显示）
1483	      const seg = c.swimLatch > 0 ? (anim.swim ?? anim.walk)
1484	        : !c.onGround ? (anim.fly ?? anim.walk)
1485	        : Math.abs(c.vx) > 0.15 ? (anim.walk ?? anim.idle) : (anim.idle ?? anim.walk);
1486	      if (seg) {
1487	        const active = Math.abs(c.vx) > 0.15 || !c.onGround || c.swimLatch > 0;
1488	        frame = seg[0] + (active ? Math.floor(c.animT / 8) % seg[1] : 0);
1489	      } else frame = 0;
1490	    } else if (c.perchState === 1 && meta.count > 1) frame = meta.count - 1;
1491	    else {
1492	      const moving = Math.abs(c.vx) > 0.15 || !c.onGround;
1493	      if (c.animLen > 0) frame = c.animBase + (moving ? Math.floor(c.animT / 8) % c.animLen : 0);
1494	      else frame = moving ? Math.floor(c.animT / 8) % meta.count : 0;
1495	    }
1496	    const fr = this.atlas.vnpc(c.def.npc, frame);
1497	    if (!fr) return;
1498	    const ctx = this.ctx;
1499	    // 缩放贴合碰撞盒；按帧最低不透明行对齐脚底（帧底透明内边距会导致悬浮）
1500	    const h = c.h * 1.25;
1501	    const w = (fr.sw / fr.sh) * h;
1502	    const pad = this.spriteBottomPad(fr, `npc${c.def.npc}:f${frame}`); // 帧内底部透明行数（按 NPC+帧号稳定缓存）
1503	    ctx.save();
1504	    ctx.translate(c.cx, c.y + c.h); // 脚底中心
1505	    ctx.scale(c.facing >= 0 ? -1 : 1, 1); // 贴图默认朝左
1506	    if (c.def.glow) {
1507	      ctx.shadowColor = 'rgba(220,255,140,0.9)';
1508	      ctx.shadowBlur = 6; // 萤火虫发光
1509	    }
1510	    ctx.drawImage(fr.img, fr.sx, fr.sy, fr.sw, fr.sh, -w / 2, -h + pad * (h / fr.sh), w, h);
1511	    ctx.restore();
1512	  }

1514	  private drawDrop(d: ItemDrop) {
1515	    const bob = Math.sin((d.age + d.bobPhase * 60) * 0.1) * 1.5;
1516	    // 优先 Maples 图标
1517	    const ar = this.atlasIcon(d.itemId);
1518	    if (ar) {
1519	      const ctx = this.ctx;
1520	      const drawH = 14 * ar.sh / ar.sw;
1521	      ctx.drawImage(ar.img, ar.sx, ar.sy, ar.sw, ar.sh, d.x, d.y + bob, 14, drawH);
1522	      // 原版 Item.color（彩色凝胶）：同贴图逐像素乘 color，XNA 预乘两步（同 NPC color pass）
1523	      if (d.color && d.color[3] > 0) {
1524	        const tinted = this.tintedSprite(`item${d.itemId}`, ar, d.color);
1525	        ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'destination-out';
1526	        ctx.globalAlpha = d.color[3] / 255;
1527	        ctx.drawImage(ar.img, ar.sx, ar.sy, ar.sw, ar.sh, d.x, d.y + bob, 14, drawH);
1528	        ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'lighter';
1529	        ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
1530	        ctx.drawImage(tinted, d.x, d.y + bob, 14, drawH);
1531	        ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
1532	      }
1533	      return;
1534	    }
1535	    const icon = this.itemIcon(d.itemId);
1536	    if (!icon) return;
1537	    this.ctx.drawImage(icon, d.x, d.y + bob, 12, 12);
1538	  }

So chains rely on the full-screen compositeLight pass instead of per-segment tinting. Hmm.

But for the WoF body, the wall covers the whole screen column — the full-screen composite would already darken it according to the light map! Let me understand compositeLight: it applies the lighting buffer over the whole scene (screen-space lightmap from LightingEngine).   vui(name: string): DrawRect | null {

  /** 按需加载 vanilla 单图(去重;失败静默)。命中返回元素,否则 null。
   *  渲染路径一律用此入口(经 vimg 别名)——直接 vimages.get 会绕过懒加载与
   *  onVImageLoaded 重烘焙钩子(棕榈树干传送消失的根因) */
  ensureVImage(file: string): HTMLImageElement | null {
    const hit = this.vimages.get(file);
    if (hit) return hit;
    if (this._iconPending.has(file)) return null;
    this._iconPending.add(file);
    const im = new Image();
    im.onload = () => {
      this.vimages.set(file, im);
      this._iconPending.delete(file);
      this.onVImageLoaded?.(file);  // 晚到的表 → ChunkCache 重烘焙
    };
    im.onerror = () => { this._iconPending.delete(file); };
    im.src = `sprites/${encodeURI(file)}`;
    return null;
  }
  /** 后台预取全部物品图标(保留兼容入口;预载流程请用 awaited 的 preloadIcons) */
  prefetchIcons(): void {
    void this.preloadIcons();

  private _iconsPromise: Promise<void> | null = null;
  /** 预载全部物品图标素材。图集化后清单 = 去重后的 ~3 张 Item_Atlas_k.png
   *  (此前 6059 张单体逐张请求);缓存 Promise——并发 await 的调用者
   *  都会等到同一批加载完成(此前旗标早退会让第二个调用者拿到假完成) */
  preloadIcons(onProgress?: (p: number) => void): Promise<void> {
    if (this._iconsPromise) return this._iconsPromise;
    const icons = [...new Set(Object.values(this.vanilla.items).map((m) => m.icon))];
    let done = 0;
    this._iconsPromise = Promise.all(icons.map((f) => new Promise<void>((resolve) => {
      if (this.vimages.has(f)) { done++; onProgress?.(done / icons.length); return resolve(); }
      const im = new Image();
      im.onload = () => { this.vimages.set(f, im); done++; onProgress?.(done / icons.length); resolve(); };
      im.onerror = () => resolve();
      im.src = `sprites/${encodeURI(f)}`;
    }))).then(() => undefined);
    return this._iconsPromise;
  }

        const value = res.zeroValue || twinSuppressed ? 0 : ctx.value;
        if (value > 0) for (const c of rollCoins(value, ctx, !!ctx.extras?.bloodMoon, this.midasT > 0)) spawn(c.id, c.n);
        // 心/星（NPCLoot_DropCommonLifeAndMana）
        for (const h of rollHeartsAndStars(ctx)) spawn(h.id, h.n);
        // Boss 药水+心（DoDeathEvents_DropBossPotionsAndHearts，boss 门 :80307）——
        // EoW 中间段 killBossFlag=false 不出（仅最后一段）
        if (this.killBossFlag && !twinSuppressed) for (const b of rollBossPotionsAndHearts(ctx)) spawn(b.id, b.n);
        }
      } else {
        for (const d of this.def.drops) {
          if (rng.next() < d.chance) {
            const n = rng.int(d.min, d.max);
            if (n > 0) game.spawnDrop(this.cx, this.cy, d.item, n, rng.range(-1.5, 1.5), -2);
          }
        }
      }
      game.onEnemyKilled(this.key, this);
      // 死亡专属声：Boss 咆哮；普通怪优先专属 killedSound，无则通用。
      // EoW 挂 killBossFlag（最后一段）——中段死只播本体 DeathSound（原版 13/14/15
      // 均为 NPCDeath1，无 Boss 咆哮）
      if (this.killBossFlag) game.playSfx('roar', 1, this.cx, this.cy);
      else if (this.def.killedSound.length) game.playSfxFiles(this.def.killedSound, 1, this.cx, this.cy);
      else game.playSfx('killed', 1, this.cx, this.cy);
      // 死亡碎裂：与主角同款物理粒子（喷溅/落地反弹/停驻淡出），颜色按怪物类型
      const [c1, c2, c3] = this.def.gore;
      const big = this.killBossFlag || this.w > 40;
      game.spawnParticles(this.cx, this.y + this.h * 0.3, c1, big ? 26 : 12, big ? 3.4 : 2.6, { life: 240, damp: 0.988, grav: 0.09 });
      game.spawnParticles(this.cx, this.cy, c2, big ? 18 : 8, big ? 2.6 : 2.0, { life: 220, damp: 0.985, grav: 0.09 });
      game.spawnParticles(this.cx, this.cy - this.h * 0.2, c3, big ? 12 : 6, big ? 3.0 : 2.3, { life: 200, damp: 0.988, grav: 0.09 });
      return true;
    }
    // 受击：少量碎屑零散溅落（不大面积）
    game.spawnParticles(this.cx, this.cy, this.def.gore[0], 5, 1.4, { life: 60 });
    return false;
  }

  /** AI 触发的标准死亡（EoW 断链自灭，NPC.cs:51876-51905 的 life=0 + HitEffect +
   *  checkDead 语义）：清无敌帧后走 hurt 全管线（掉落/记账/音效/粒子）——
   *  原版直改 life 不经 StrikeNPC，故 iframes 不应阻塞 */
  die(game: GameHooks): void {
    this.hp = 0;
    this.iframes = 0;
    this.hurt(this.maxHp + 9999, 0, 0, game);
  }

  draw() { /* Renderer 统一绘制 */ }
}

    this.props.length = 0;

  }

  all(): Entity[] {
    return [...this.enemies, ...this.drops, ...this.npcs, ...this.projectiles, ...this.props, ...this.critters];
  }
}

```

      let ny = p.y + p.vy;

          p.vy = -p.vy * 0.35; // 溅落反弹
          p.vx *= 0.6;
        } else {
          p.vy = 0; p.vx *= 0.5; p.grav = 0; // 停驻地表
        }
        ny = p.y;
      }
      p.x = nx; p.y = ny;

    opts: { life?: number; damp?: number; grav?: number; size?: number } = {},
  ) {
    const base = opts.life ?? 40;
    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
      const a = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
      const life = Math.round(base * (0.6 + Math.random() * 0.4));
      this.particles.push({
        x, y, vx: Math.cos(a) * speed * Math.random(), vy: Math.sin(a) * speed * Math.random() - 1,
        life, maxLife: life, color, size: opts.size ?? 2 + Math.random() * 2,
        damp: opts.damp, grav: opts.grav,
      });
    }
    if (this.particles.length > 600) this.particles.splice(0, this.particles.length - 600);
  }

private drawWoFBody(head: Enemy, top: number, camRX: number, camRY: number, viewW: number, viewH: number, z: number) {
  const img = this.atlas ? this.atlas.ensureVImage('vanilla/WallOfFlesh.png') : null;
  if (!img || !img.height) return;
  const num = Math.floor(img.height / 3);            // 140(:37829)
  const screenTop = camRY - viewH / (2 * z);          // Main.screenPosition.Y
  const screenBottom = camRY + viewH / (2 * z);       // screenPosition.Y + screenHeight(:37833 覆写)
  let top2 = top;                                     // wofDrawAreaTop
  let n4 = Math.floor((top2 - screenTop) / num) + 1;  // :37836
  if (n4 > 12) return;                                // :37838
  const n5 = n4 * num;
  if (n5 > 0) top2 -= n5;                             // :37841-37844
  let x = head.x;                                     // npc.position.X(:37850)
  if (head.facing > 0) x -= 80;                       // direction>0 → -80
  const flip = head.facing === 1;                     // spriteDirection==1 → FlipHorizontally(:37856-37858;AI :26041 spriteDirection=direction)
  const frameRow = Math.floor(wofDrawFrameIndex / 6) * num;  // :37860
  wofDrawFrameIndex = (wofDrawFrameIndex + 1) % 18;          // :37861-37863(每 draw 调用推进,原版同)
  const ctx = this.ctx;
  const W = img.width;
  ctx.save();
  if (flip) { ctx.translate(2 * x + W, 0); ctx.scale(-1, 1); }
  for (let i = Math.floor(top2); i < screenBottom; i += num) {   // :37868
    let n8 = screenBottom - i;
    if (n8 > num) n8 = num;
    for (let j = 0; j < n8; j += 16) {                           // :37873
      ctx.drawImage(img, 0, frameRow + j, W, 16, x, i + j, W, 16);
    }
  }
  ctx.restore();
}

Now the rope (:37879-37928):

```ts
private drawWoFRope(head: Enemy, h: Enemy, top: number, bottom: number) {
  const chain = this.atlas ? this.atlas.vmisc('vanilla/Chain12.png') : null;
  if (!chain) return;
  const scale = (h.vanilla?.scale ?? 1) * h.vanillaScale;
  const mx = head.x + head.w / 2;                     // :37884
  const anchorY = top + (bottom - top) * h.ai0;       // :37890-37891
  const phase = ((Math.floor(h.animT) % 15) + 15) % 15; // frameCounter 0..14(case 115 循环)
  let flip = phase > 7;                               // :37887 frameCounter>7
  const x0 = h.cx;                                    // :37893 Center.X
  const y0 = h.y + h.h - h.h * 0.5 * scale;           // :37894 Bottom.Y - height*0.5*scale
  let vx = x0, vy = y0;                               // + netOffset(本地 0)
  let dx = mx - vx, dy = anchorY - vy;
  const rot = Math.atan2(dy, dx) - Math.PI / 2;       // :37899
  const ctx = this.ctx;
  let guard = 0;
  let running = true;
  while (running && guard++ < 100) {
    let height = 28;                                  // :37905
    const step = Math.floor(40 * scale);              // num5 :37906(40*scale)
    let dist = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
    if (dist < step) { height = Math.floor(dist) - step + 28; running = false; }  // :37908-37910
    const k = (28 * scale) / dist;                    // :37912 (28*scale/dist)
    dx *= k; dy *= k;
    vx += dx; vy += dy;
    dx = mx - vx; dy = anchorY - vy;                  // :37914-37915
    ctx.save();
    ctx.translate(vx, vy);
    ctx.rotate(rot);
    if (flip) ctx.scale(-1, 1);                       // SpriteEffects.FlipHorizontally 交替
    flip = !flip;
    ctx.drawImage(chain.img, 0, 0, chain.sw, height, -chain.sw / 2, -chain.sh / 2, chain.sw, height);
    ctx.restore();
  }
}
```

```ts
private drawWoFTongue(head: Enemy, p: Player) {
  const chain = this.atlas ? this.atlas.vmisc('vanilla/Chain12.png') : null;
  if (!chain) return;
  const mx = head.x + head.w / 2;
  const my = head.y + head.h / 2;
  let vx = p.x + p.w * 0.5, vy = p.y + p.h * 0.5;
  let dx = mx - vx, dy = my - vy;
  const rot = Math.atan2(dy, dx) - Math.PI / 2;
  const ctx = this.ctx;
  let guard = 0;
  let running = true;
  while (running && guard++ < 100) {
    let dist = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
    if (dist < 40) { running = false; continue; }      // :37947-37951
    const k = chain.sh / dist;                          // Chain12.Height()/dist
    dx *= k; dy *= k;
    vx += dx; vy += dy;
    dx = mx - vx; dy = my - vy;
    ctx.save();
    ctx.translate(vx, vy);
    ctx.rotate(rot);
    ctx.drawImage(chain.img, 0, 0, chain.sw, chain.sh, -chain.sw / 2, -chain.sh / 2, chain.sw, chain.sh);
    ctx.restore();
  }
}
```

DrawWoF orchestration:

```ts
private drawWoF(player: Player, entities: Entity[], camRX, camRY, viewW, viewH, z, world) {
  if (!this.atlas) return;
  const head = (entities as Enemy[]).find(e => !e.dead && e.vanillaId === 113 && e.hp > 0) ?? null;
  if (head) {
    this.wofHead = head;
    // draw area 近似(见注释)
    const { top, bottom } = this.wofDrawArea(head, entities as Enemy[]);
    if (player && !player.dead && player.buffs.has(BuffType.TheTongue)) this.drawWoFTongue(head, player);
    for (const e of entities as Enemy[]) if (!e.dead && e.vanilla?.aiStyle === 29) this.drawWoFRope(head, e, top, bottom);
    this.drawWoFBody(head, top, camRX, camRY, viewW, viewH, z);
  } else if (this.wofHead) {
    // 死亡检测(详见下)
    const dead = this.wofHead;
    this.wofHead = null;
    if (dead.dead && !dead.bossFled) this.spawnWoFDeathFx(dead, player, world, camRX, camRY, viewW, z);
  }
}
```

wofDrawArea derivation:

```ts
private wofDrawAreaOf(head: Enemy, entities: Enemy[]): { top: number; bottom: number } {
  let eyeTop: Enemy | null = null, eyeBot: Enemy | null = null;
  for (const e of entities) {
    if (e.dead || e.vanillaId !== 114) continue;
    if (e.ai0 > 0) eyeTop = e; else eyeBot = e;
  }
  if (eyeTop && eyeBot) {
    return { top: 2 * eyeTop.cy - head.cy, bottom: 2 * eyeBot.cy - head.cy };
  }
  return { top: head.cy - 160, bottom: head.cy + 160 };   // 兜底:最小跨度 160
}
```

Simulate & draw:
```ts
private stepWoFDeathFx() {
  const list = this.wofDeathFx;
  for (const p of list) {
    p.life--;
    p.vy += p.grav ?? 0.1;
    if (p.damp) { p.vx *= p.damp; p.vy *= p.damp; }
    p.x += p.vx; p.y += p.vy;
  }
  // 压缩
  let n = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < list.length; i++) if (list[i].life > 0) list[n++] = list[i];
  list.length = n;
  const ctx = this.ctx;
  for (const p of list) {
    ctx.globalAlpha = Math.max(0, p.life / p.maxLife);
    ctx.fillStyle = p.color;
    ctx.fillRect(p.x - p.size / 2, p.y - p.size / 2, p.size, p.size);
  }
  ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
}
```

function st(): NpcGridFrameState { return { counter: 0, idx: 0 }; }

/** 驱动 n tick，收集每 tick 的帧索引 */
function run(fn: () => number, n: number): number[] {
  const out: number[] = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) out.push(fn());
  return out;
}
/** 状态序列去重压缩（保留顺序）——校验"持帧→推进"节奏用 */
function compress(seq: number[]): number[] {
  return seq.filter((v, i) => i === 0 || v !== seq[i - 1]);
}

// Boss AI 冒烟:骷髅王(双手)/史莱姆王/克脑(爬行者)核心行为采样
import { Enemy } from '../src/entities/Enemy.ts';
import { TileStore } from '../src/world/TileStore.ts';
import { World } from '../src/world/World.ts';

const st = new TileStore(80, 200);
const world = new World(80, 200, 1, 't');
world.store = st;
for (let x = 0; x < 80; x++) st.setTileSilent(x, 150, 1); // 地板
const enemies: Enemy[] = [];
const game = {
  world,
  player: { cx: 40 * 16, cy: 140 * 16, x: 40 * 16 - 10, y: 140 * 16, w: 20, h: 42, dead: false } as never,
  spawnDrop: () => null, damagePlayer: () => {}, addDamageNumber: () => {}, cutTile: () => {},
  onEnemyKilled: () => {}, spawnEnemy: () => {}, enemies: () => enemies,
  spawnParticles: () => {}, notifyInventoryChanged: () => {}, playSfx: () => {}, playSfxFiles: () => {},
  showPickupLabel: () => {},
  // bossAI.addEnemy 读 game.entities.add/nextId(与真实 EntityManager 同构)
  entities: (() => {
    const wrap = { nextId: 2, add: (e: Enemy, _b?: string) => { e.id = wrap.nextId++; enemies.push(e); } };

/** 就地剔除 dead 实体(保持顺序,零分配) */
function compact(list: Entity[]): void {
  let w = 0;
  for (let r = 0; r < list.length; r++) {
    const e = list[r];
    if (!e.dead) list[w++] = e;
  }
  list.length = w;
}

export class EntityManager {
  nextId = 1;
  enemies: Entity[] = [];
  drops: Entity[] = [];
  npcs: Entity[] = [];
  projectiles: Entity[] = [];
  props: Entity[] = [];   // 场景物件（墓碑等不可拾取物理实体）
  critters: Entity[] = []; // 小动物（无害被动生物）

  add(e: Entity, bucket: 'enemies' | 'drops' | 'npcs' | 'projectiles' | 'props' | 'critters') {
    e.id = this.nextId++;
    this[bucket].push(e);
  }

  update(dt: number, game: GameHooks) {
    // 就地压缩替代 6×filter(2026-08 审计 G9:每 tick 6 个新数组,60Hz 恒定分配)
    for (const list of [this.enemies, this.drops, this.npcs, this.projectiles, this.props, this.critters]) {
      for (const e of list) e.fixedUpdate(dt, game);
      compact(list);
    }
  }

  clear() {
    this.enemies.length = 0;
    this.drops.length = 0;
    this.npcs.length = 0;
    this.projectiles.length = 0;
    this.props.length = 0;
    this.critters.length = 0;
  }

att < 12 && r === null; att++) {\n  try {\n    await page.goto(`${ORIGIN}/?play=small`, { waitUntil: 'domcontentloaded', timeout: 90000 });\n    r = await page.evaluate(() => new Promise((res, rej) => {\n      const t0 = Date.now();\n      const iv = setInterval(async () => {\n        if (window.__swGame?.player) {\n          clearInterval(iv);\n          try {\n            const g = window.__swGame;\n            const boss = await import('/src/entities/bossAI.ts');\n            const { BuffType } = await import('/src/stats/Buffs.ts');\n            const out = {};\n            // 玩家右侧 400px 生成墙(墙向玩家推进;direction 由 vx==0 首帧按玩家侧定向)\n            const head = boss.spawnVanillaEnemy(g, 113, Math.round(g.player.x) + 400, Math.round(g.player.y));\n            out.headSpawned = !!head;\n            if (!head) { res(out); return; }\n            out.headBox = [head.x, head.y, head.w, head.h];\n            // 舌头:玩家上狂卷之舌 buff(:37818 门 tongued)\n            g.player.buffs.apply(BuffType.TheTongue, 10);\n            out.tongued = g.player.buffs.has(BuffType.TheTongue);\n            // 等 60 tick:部件生成 + draw area 平滑起步\n            await new Promise((r2) => setTimeout(r2, 1500));\n            const ents = g.entities.all();\n            out.eyes = ents.filter((e) => e.vanillaId === 114 && !e.dead).length;\n            out.hungry = ents.filter((e) => e.vanillaId === 115 && !e.dead).length;\n            const h2 = ents.find((e) => e.vanillaId === 113);\n            out.headStillAlive = !!(h2 && !h2.dead);\n            out.headPos = h2 ? [Math.round(h2.x), Math.round(h2.y), h2.facing] : null;\n            // 相机跟玩家,墙应在屏内(屏宽 1280/1.25 ≈ 1024 世界 px,墙距 400px)\n            await new Promise((r3) => setTimeout(r3, 1500));\n            res(out);\n          } catch (e) { rej(String(e && e.message || e)); }\n        } else if (Date.now() - t0 > 120000) {\n          clearInterval(iv); rej('boot timeout');\n        }\n      }, 200);\n    }));\n  } catch (e) { console.log(`attempt ${att} failed: ${e}`); r = null; }\n}\nif (!r) { console.log('PROBE FAILED: no result'); await browser.close(); process.exit(1); }\n// 截图:墙推进中(嘴/眼/饥饿者 + 墙身同屏)\nawait new Promise((r2) => setTimeout(r2, 2500));\nawait page.screenshot({ path: '/tmp/wof-body-1.png' });\n// 像素断言:墙列(头 x 附近 190px 宽)中段应有大量非透明/非天空像素(墙身贴图覆盖)\nconst pix = await page.evaluate(() => {\n  const cv = document.querySelector('canvas');\n  const g = window.__swGame;\n  const head = g.entities.all().find((e) => e.vanillaId === 113 && !e.dead);\n  if (!head) return { err: 'head gone' };\n  const cam = g.camera ?? g.cam;\n  const z = cam ? cam.zoom : 1.25;\n  const viewW = cv.width, viewH = cv.height;\n  // 世界 → 屏幕:屏心 + (世界-相机)×z\n  const sx = Math.round(viewW / 2 + (head.x - (cam ? cam.x : head.x)) * z);\n  const sy = Math.round(viewH / 2 + (head.cy - (cam ? cam.y : head.cy)) * z);\n  const ctx = cv.getContext('2d');\n  const x0 = Math.max(0, sx - 10), w = Math.min(190, cv.width - x0);\n  const y0 = Math.max(0, sy - 200), h = Math.min(400, cv.height - y0);\n  if (w <= 0 || h <= 0) return { err: 'region offscreen', sx, sy };\n  const data = ctx.getImageData(x0, y0, w, h).data;\n  let flesh = 0, total = 0, sumR = 0, sumG = 0, sumB = 0;\n  for (let i = 0; i < data.length; i += 4) {\n    const a = data[i + 3];\n    if (a < 10) continue;\n    total++;\n    const rr = data[i], gg = data[i + 1], bb = data[i + 2];\n    sumR += rr; sumG += gg; sumB += bb;\n    // 肉墙贴图是红棕暗色:R 明显大于 B 且偏暗\n    if (rr > bb + 15 && rr < 200) flesh++;\n  }\n  return {\n    sx, sy, zoom: z, total,\n    flesh, fleshPct: total ? Math.round((flesh / total) * 100) : 0,\n    avgRGB: total ? [Math.round(sumR / total), Math.round(sumG / total), Math.round(sumB / total)] : null,\n  };\n});\nconsole.log(JSON.stringify({ ...r, pix }, null, 2));\nawait browser.close();\n"
}

```ts
private wofDrawAreaOf(head: Enemy, entities: Enemy[]): { top: number; bottom: number } {
  let eyeTop: Enemy | null = null;
  let eyeBot: Enemy | null = null;
  for (const e of entities) {
    if (e.dead || e.vanillaId !== 114) continue;
    if (e.ai0 > 0) eyeTop = e; else eyeBot = e;
  }
  // mid = head.cy 精确(:25955-25956 吸附式);跨度由双眼间距反解——
  // 上眼 cy=(mid+top)/2、下眼 cy=(mid+bottom)/2 → span=2×(下眼-上眼)。
  // 用间距而非单眼位置:双眼共享同一滞后(生成锚点差 h/2 + ±1 步进),
  // 差分后相消,仅剩微分滞后(静态空腔下双眼均已收敛 → 精确)
  if (eyeTop && eyeBot) {
    let span = 2 * (eyeBot.cy - eyeTop.cy);
    if (span < 160) span = 160;   // :25940-25946 最小跨度兜底
    return { top: head.cy - span / 2, bottom: head.cy + span / 2 };
  }
  return { top: head.cy - 80, bottom: head.cy + 80 };
}
```

Math.round(lum / total) : null };\n            };\n            const wx = h.facing > 0 ? h.x - 80 : h.x;\n            out.A_aboveTop = region(wx + 30, top - 60, 130, 50);    // 顶之上:期望非肉色\n            out.A_belowTop = region(wx + 30, top + 10, 130, 50);    // 顶之下:期望肉色\n            out.B_belowHead = region(wx + 30, h.cy + 150, 130, 150); // 头下方:期望肉色(画到屏底)\n            // C) 舌头:嘴→玩家连线中点 vs 偏移对照(均在墙列之外)\n            const mx = h.x + h.w / 2, my = h.y + h.h / 2;\n            const px = g.player.x + g.player.w / 2, py = g.player.y + g.player.h / 2;\n            const tmx = (mx + px) / 2, tmy = (my + py) / 2;\n            out.C_tongueOn = region(tmx - 12, tmy - 12, 24, 24);\n            out.C_tongueOff = region(tmx - 12, tmy - 92, 24, 24);   // 垂直偏 80px\n            out.C_tongueOff2 = region(tmx - 12, tmy + 68, 24, 24);\n            // 饥饿者在场数(链的目标)\n            out.hungry = g.entities.all().filter((e) => e.vanillaId === 115 && !e.dead).length;\n            out.tongued = g.player.buffs.has(BuffType.TheTongue);\n            res(out);\n          } catch (e) { rej(String(e && e.message || e)); }\n        } else if (Date.now() - t0 > 120000) { clearInterval(iv); rej('boot timeout'); }\n      }, 200);\n    }));\n  } catch (e) { console.log(`attempt ${att} failed: ${e}`); r = null; }\n}\nconsole.log(JSON.stringify(r, null, 2));\nawait browser.close();\n"

DrawWOFBody tiling algorithm key points (for the report):
- num = texture height/3 = 140 per animation frame.
- Bottom bound: vanilla assigns wofDrawAreaBottom then immediately overwrites with screenPosition.Y + screenHeight → the body tiles down to the SCREEN bottom, independent of the cavity bottom.
- Row alignment: rows = floor((top − screenTop)/140) + 1; top -= rows*140 → the first row start ≡ top (mod 140) in world space, so rows tile seamlessly and stay world-anchored while the camera pans (no swimming); rows > 12 (top more than ~1680px below screen top) skips the draw entirely.
- Within each 140px row: 16px-tall horizontal strips (last row clamped to the remaining height), each drawn as source rect (0, frameRow + j, 190, 16) at world (x, i+j).
- X = head hitbox left, −80 when direction > 0; FlipHorizontally when spriteDirection == 1 (AI :26041 sets spriteDirection = direction → facing).
- Animation: frame row = (wofDrawFrameIndex/6)*140, counter incremented after use, wraps at 18 → 3 frames × 6 steps.
- Per-strip Lighting.GetColor is covered by the engine's compositeLight full-screen multiply (same light buffer).

Let me also mention the vanilla quirk notes:
- DrawWOFRopeToTheHungry draws TextureAssets.Chain12 but sizes the source rect/origin with Chain4's dimensions — both 16×28, numerically identical, noted in a comment.
- The rope's terminating segment can go non-positive in vanilla (XNA would throw); guarded here.

Also worth reporting: the probe found a pre-existing quirk in bossAI_wof.ts (outside my scope): the eyes are spawned with a bottom-anchor (y = target − h) while the chase target uses a center-anchor (− h/2), leaving a persistent 60px offset that the ±1 chase erodes at 1px/tick — my span-based derivation is immune to it, but the eye sprites themselves lag their vanilla targets. And in solid rock the draw area pins to the min span 160.

I think that's a complete report. Let me write it concisely.
